APPENDIX I. KOWLOON AND LAPPA: QUARTERLY RETURNS OF TRADE. APRIL*JUNE 1897.
192 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [APRIL-JUNE, (&#39;Becewd 14th July 1897.) CUSTOM HOUSE, KOWLOON, zoTH JULY 1897. S IR, I.-a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 387, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring stations in Native vessels for the second quarter of the year t897, April-June. b. the Custom House (*E f ) was opened on the 2nd April 1887, and the collection of Provincial Likin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, began on that day. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Bk.2h 3o and Hkft 8o per picul) commenced on the i4th April 1887, and the collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, on the ist July 7887. The stations at present controlled by the Kowloon Customs are known as the Capsuimoon ( P 3), Changehow (9 ), Fotochow (0 o 08), and Kowloon (:L i) stations. c. The word &quot; Inwards &quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China. II. 1897. LAPPA 03 (Received 19th JuY 1897.) CUSTOM HOUS, LAPPA, 12TH JULY 1897. SIR, I.-a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the second quarter of the year 1897, April-June. b. The Custom House (4 ) was opened on the 2nd April 1887. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.2k 30 and Rk.T 2 o per picul) and the collection of Provincial Likin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, com- menced on that day. The collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, commenced on the rat July 1887. The stations at present controlled by the Lappa Customs are known as the Malowchow ( fq) and Chienshan (V 0j) stations, with three sub-stations, known as the Shekkok (5 A), Kuttai (7 - ), and Kwanchiap ( ) sub-stations. c. The word &quot; Inwards &quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot; Outwards,&quot; from China. II.
